# Movement Studio Best-Practice Architecture Plan

Last reviewed: 2026-07-07
Status: active architecture-hardening plan and standing audit board.
Scope: keep Posture Studio / Game Studio movement code on a maintainable motion-engine path for visible, believable child gameplay before expanding broader human movement support.

## Current Standing Board

Current audit timestamp: 2026-07-07, Codex documentation/code audit on branch `dev`.

How closely the implementation is sticking to the plan: very close for the current standing/Game Studio architecture, with one narrow root-motion support gain now proved, and intentionally incomplete for broader human movement. Code inspection and the focused gates show the important shape is intact: `MovementSourceFrame` stays raw, `resolveMovementMotionFrame` is the shared source/display motion pipe, Replay and Game scoring use the same event contract, Game visual proof is frame-selected and reviewed, user-facing coverage is explicit, and avatar renderer/pipeline hotspots are guarded by line-count and purity checks. The biggest current gaps are proof depth for the next movement families and durable proof-artifact handoff, not architecture direction.

Current verified support claims:

- User-facing: `upright`, `upper-body-standing`, `standing-side-bend-head-direction`, `squat-knee-lift`, and narrow standing `root-turn`.
- Internal preview/demo-only and now explicitly testable through synthetic proof fixtures: `root-travel`, `walking`, `pivot-weight-transfer`, `jump-hop`, `lunges`, `sitting`, `kneeling`, `lying-floor-work`, `quadruped`, `rolling-crawling`, `yoga`, `pilates`, and `props-contact`.
- Internal diagnostic/demo-only: `facing-occlusion`.
- Still blocked at the registry level: none; every family has an explicit supported, approximate, or diagnostic state.
- Not user-facing: walking, root travel, broad full-body exercise coverage, and all preview-ready floor/yoga/Pilates/chair/lunge/jump/prop families until they get recorded replay proof plus Game visual proof. The current root-turn claim is narrow standing root orientation only; it is not walking, turning while traveling, or full locomotion support.

Current movement-family coverage:

- Registry families: 19.
- User-facing supported families: 5/19.
- Non-user-facing internal families: 14/19.
- Internal approximate preview/demo families: 13/19.
- Internal diagnostic/demo families: 1/19.
- Blocked/unsupported families: 0/19.
- Testable preview coverage: 19/19 families have an explicit user-facing, internal-preview, or internal-diagnostic path.
- Full production support remains much lower than preview coverage because 14 families still lack full recorded proof.

The next useful walking gain is a deliberate walk/root-travel recording followed by the generated `movement-proof-root-travel` quick-validation path rather than another generic audit pass.
- The generated broad capture-contract lifecycle still carries historical/stale metadata in ignored `tmp/` artifacts. Future cleanup should recompute current support status in preflight or clearly label embedded status as historical.
- Existing support is proven on selected recordings and selected Game visual frames. Any motion-frame, display-mirror, support-presentation, or avatar-rendering change must refresh the reviewed analysis, captures/review decisions as needed, and `movement:architecture-guard`.
- Keep broad movement-family expansion on the shared Replay/Game motion path. Do not patch Game Studio with one-off family rules that bypass Replay proof.

## Executive Verdict

The implementation is following the plan closely in shape, but it is not done and should not be treated as done. The other agent has moved the work in the right direction: live, recorded, and synthetic inputs now feed shared source/motion contracts; Game Studio routes pass motion frames into the avatars; scoring uses shared gameplay events; replay proof tooling is much stronger; and many avatar runtime helpers have been extracted.

This is a current-checkout audit, not a merged-state audit. The branch is `dev`. Earlier on 2026-07-07 this continuation started with a large dirty movement worktree, but the architecture helper/test slice has since been committed and `git ls-files --others --exclude-standard 'src/app/(dashboard)/demos/movements/**'` now reports 0 untracked movement source/test files. The remaining local proof artifacts under `tmp/movement-replay-lab/**` are ignored scratch. Treat the percentages below as the state of this checkout and re-audit after any further movement-family promotion, proof-artifact refresh, or documentation change.

Current handoff audit on 2026-07-07: the worktree already contained movement-related local edits before this documentation cleanup, including the plan, proof scripts, package scripts, coverage/proof files, and two untracked replay export helpers. Direct code inspection and the cheap executable gates support the same headline: the standing/Game Studio architecture has stayed close to this plan, but broader human movement support remains intentionally blocked behind proof.

## Plain-English Product Status

This is a user-facing app, so "supported" means the movement is safe to present to users as something the game can recognize and show clearly enough for child gameplay. It does not mean perfect biomechanics, and it does not mean every possible human movement works.

Current user-facing movement families:

- `upright`: neutral standing/posture readiness.
- `standing-side-bend-head-direction`: standing side bend plus head-direction readability.
- `squat-knee-lift`: standing squat and single-knee-lift support.
- `upper-body-standing`: scoped standing upper-body support for arm raise, reach, twist, and shoulder/scapula proxy presentation.
- `root-turn`: narrow standing root orientation/turn readability, without root travel or walking.

Current non-user-facing or internal-only movement families:

- `root-travel` remains internal/demo-only.
- `walking`, `pivot-weight-transfer`, `jump-hop`, `lunges`, `sitting`, `kneeling`, `lying-floor-work`, `quadruped`, `rolling-crawling`, `yoga`, `pilates`, and `props-contact` are internal preview/demo-ready with synthetic proof coverage.
- `facing-occlusion` is internal diagnostic/demo-ready for root-heading/away-body fallback checks, but remains non-user-facing.
- Broader lower-body/floor families remain non-user-facing until they get the same recorded proof and Game visual proof as the standing families.

The important product rule is: do not show or describe an unsupported movement as supported. If a family is user-facing, the reviewed manifest, visual captures, semantic review, and `movement:architecture-guard` must agree. If any of those drift after a motion/rendering change, the family should be treated as needing re-proof before the support claim is trusted again.

Allowed user-facing claim today:

- The game can support standing posture, side bend/head direction, squat/single-knee-lift, scoped standing upper-body arm raise/reach/twist movements, and narrow standing root-turn readability.
- The avatar should visibly follow broad child-readable motion for those scoped standing families.
- Tracking uncertainty or source limitations should degrade gracefully; they should not be presented as the child's failure.

Not allowed in product/UI copy yet:

- Do not claim full-body exercise coverage.
- Do not claim walking, jumping, lunges, floor/yoga/Pilates, crawling/rolling, sitting, kneeling, props/contact, root travel, turning while traveling, or full locomotion production support.
- Internal preview/demo-ready means "available for focused testing and proof capture", not "safe to advertise to users".
- Do not imply clinical precision, exact joint measurement, medical assessment, or complete biomechanics.

If proof drifts:

- Treat the affected family as needing re-proof before expanding copy or demos.
- Re-run the focused reviewed analysis, Game visual proof, semantic review, and `movement:architecture-guard`.
- Reopen this plan section if the guard loses a passing bundle, loses readable Game proof, or moves a user-facing family back to missing/manual/product-scope proof.

- `movement:upper-body-standing-capture-preflight` now checks the generated broad capture contract without running the final support audit, so a recording-bound handoff can report schema issues, staged workflow progress, the next workflow command id and command, missing reviewed artifacts, stale focused Game plans, or unfilled semantic decisions. Current caveat: preflight reports embedded contract metadata, which can be stale even when the final contract-driven audit recomputes from artifact paths and passes.
- Accepted source-data and product-scope limitations are limitations, not passes.
- Most non-upright movement families remain approximate, internal preview, diagnostic, or synthetic-only.
- Working-tree hygiene is no longer the main gap after the focused commits; keep generated proof artifacts treated as ignored scratch unless a compact reviewed summary is promoted into docs.

Progress estimates:

- Overall full human-movement engine: about 70%.
- Current standing/posture/Game Studio slice: 98%.
- Architecture-hardening slice: 99%.
- Current proof-closure slice: 100% for the current supported standing scope; the reviewed manifest still has 0 blocking rows, and the refreshed 50-frame Game visual capture/review set matches the reviewed analysis.
- Current Game parity proof slice: 100% for the current supported standing scope; score/message parity is proved, deterministic Game visual-proof targets are present in the reviewed analysis, a machine-readable target plan exists, Game Studio has a debug-only recorded-frame injection route, semantic review passes the current 50/50 selected captures, the capture manifest is checked against the current reviewed analysis for missing/stale/context-mismatched target rows, the semantic review is checked against the capture manifest for missing/stale/context-mismatched decision rows, the `squat-knee-lift` support-claim audit gates that product claim, the upper-body readiness audit gates the scoped upper-body claims, and the current reviewed artifacts still show 11,383 score/message parity frames with 0 divergence frames.
- Movement-family preview coverage slice: 100% testable preview/diagnostic coverage, 26% user-facing production support.
- Seated validation lane: 77%; opt-in recorded proof rows, a support-readiness audit with recording/review target split, a full generated Replay/Game proof chain, a best-partial handoff shortcut, corrected root-motion jump diagnostics, depth-aware seated orientation, relative seated-hinge classification, analyzer-level seated-forward-fold candidate scoring, seated-only candidate-amplitude gating, explicit `0.000/1.000` audit output, seated leg-extension/leg-lift analyzer plus Game proof, wider-export blocker attribution, a `movement-proof-seated-forward-fold` recording/quick-validation plan, top-level architecture-guard next-recording output, path-aware support recording-plan writing, and an architecture-guard non-promotion backstop now exist. The current best partial bundle has reviewed Replay proof for `seated-neutral`, `seated-twist`, `seated-leg-lift`, and `chair-contact`, plus focused Game readable-pass review for `strongest-seated-chair-contact`, `strongest-seated-twist`, and `strongest-seated-leg-lift`; all currently exported recordings still measure seated-forward-fold below threshold at `0.000/1.000`, so forward-fold analyzer/Game proof is still missing.
- Walking validation lane: 41%; `movement:expansion-preview-handoff:walking` now generates a focused walk/step recording checklist and Replay/Game proof command chain, its analyzer command uses `--include-walking-product-scope-proof` so `root-travel` can be deliberately validated without changing default product truth, `movement:walking-support-audit` gives a hard support-readiness answer with missing-analyzer reasons, best candidate-vs-required amplitude, blocker counts, and a path-aware `movement-proof-root-travel` recording/quick-validation plan, and `movement:architecture-guard` now enforces the non-promotion invariant while walking is internal-preview while also printing a next recording command that resolves against the generated support plan. Current result is blocked: `root-travel` remains a product-scope limitation in the default reviewed gate but becomes an explicit below-threshold recording task in the promotion plan, the stricter audit reports 0 analyzer-evidence proof cases, the focused current candidate is only `0.002/0.160`, no recording has passed `root-travel` as a walking proof case, and `strongest-root-travel` is missing from the current Game visual plan/readability review.
- Average progress across the 15 plan sections: about 90%.
- Plan adherence for the current standing architecture: 97%.

The headline answer to "how closely have they stuck to it": close enough that the foundation is recognizable, not close enough to relax. The current work should continue to be judged by proof manifests, focused visual capture, and renderer-boundary size, not by the number of extracted helper files.

## Product Target

This is gameplay mirroring for children aged roughly 6-14, not clinical biomechanics.

The intended experience:

```text
Child moves
  -> avatar visibly copies the broad movement
  -> game rewards effort and recognizable motion
  -> tracking failures degrade gracefully
```

The target is not perfect joint-angle measurement. A squat does not need medical-grade knee math, but the avatar must visibly squat. A lean does not need exact spinal geometry, but the avatar must lean the right way. A leg raise does not need clinical precision, but it must read as the correct leg lifting rather than a broken crouch.

## Target Architecture

All input types should normalize into one standard source contract before movement decisions:

```text
live webcam adapter      -> MovementSourceFrame
recorded replay adapter  -> MovementSourceFrame
synthetic proof adapter  -> MovementSourceFrame
  -> shared movement pipe
  -> get-ready countdown and calibration gate
  -> normalized truth skeleton
  -> camera confidence and uncertainty state
  -> movement confidence, orientation, support/contact model
  -> explicit mirror/display side mapping
  -> child-friendly scoring and motivation events
  -> retarget frame and owner decisions
  -> avatar application targets
  -> thin VRM/Three.js application layer
  -> replay eval coverage gate
  -> replay/game parity proof
```

Allowed differences after `MovementSourceFrame`:

- UI presentation.
- scoring screen state.
- player/instructor labels.
- route progression.
- debug display.
- documented avatar identity/profile differences.

Not allowed after `MovementSourceFrame`:

- separate Replay-only or Game-only movement decisions.
- separate player lower-body ownership rules.
- separate support/contact solving.
- separate readability amplification.
- separate retarget eligibility.
- separate fallback behavior when confidence drops.
- avatar bone behavior that is not clearly avatar-profile or rest-pose application.

- Focused proof: `movementRootMotion.test.ts` now covers true jump flight/landing, raised feet without root lift, and weak/hidden feet. `movementReplayAnalyzer.test.ts` keeps analyzer jump diagnostics aligned with the same root-lift contract.
- Seated focused analyzer result for `px736zs97w9axrn39je7pfahc989q8jv`: `root intent ... jump 0`, down from the earlier 1,290 false jump frames and the intermediate 776 frames after the first reliable-feet-only guard.

- Overall full human-movement engine moves from about 48% to about 49%. Phase 12 moves from about 96% to 97%, and Phase 14 moves from about 76% to 77%. Seated validation lane stays 52% because the missing proof cases are still missing.

## Recommended Next Slice

The safest next slice is recording or identifying seated forward-fold evidence, then doing the same for a real walking/root-travel bundle. The focused 50-target Game visual proof set is now refreshed and guard-green; keep it current as a regression gate rather than treating recapture as the main product gain.

Why this is the best next gain:

1. Current reviewed proof has no active manifest blockers: 153 rows, 65 covered-by-other-recording, 0 failed, 0 missing-proof, 0 manual-review, 9 product-scope-limitation, 18 source-data-limitation, and 27 accepted limitations.
2. `standing-side-bend-head-direction`, broad `upper-body-standing`, and `squat-knee-lift` are now promoted and guarded by the fast architecture gate.
3. Broad `upper-body-standing` has one scoped passing top-candidate bundle, focused Replay proof-set review, focused Game arm-raise/reach/twist capture, and semantic readable-pass review.
4. `movement:expansion-preview-audit` proves the broader internal preview/diagnostic families are internally testable through shared synthetic pose/support/presentation/root-motion logic, including walking as an alternating step-response preview and facing/occlusion as a diagnostic-only path. `sitting` also has a generated recorded-proof handoff plus opt-in proof-manifest rows.
5. The immediate proof risk is still artifact freshness after future code changes, but the current 50-target Game visual capture/review set is green.
6. Record or identify seated forward-fold evidence, use the generated `movement-proof-seated-forward-fold` quick-validation command, run the generated handoff commands, capture/review Replay and Game visual targets, rerun `--include-seated-product-scope-proof`, and only then consider promotion.
7. Record or identify a real walking/root-travel bundle. The current best wider-export root-travel amplitude is about `0.0033`, and the current focused walking handoff candidate is only `0.002/0.160`, so the blocker is missing source motion, not threshold tuning. Use the generated `movement-proof-root-travel` fresh recording scenario and quick-validation command from `movement:walking-support-audit`.
8. `root-travel` should stay scoped out because its 9 rows are intentionally `product-scope-limitation`.

Next concrete tasks:

1. Record or identify seated forward-fold evidence, then run `npx -p node@22.13.0 npm run movement:replay:validate-scenario -- --scenario movement-proof-seated-forward-fold --quiet` once the fresh recording is available in the reviewed recording plan/export, followed by the generated sitting handoff chain through Replay review, Game capture, semantic review, and `movement:sitting-support-audit`.
2. Current user-facing support list in product/UI copy: `upright`, `upper-body-standing`, `standing-side-bend-head-direction`, `squat-knee-lift`, and narrow standing `root-turn` for standing root orientation only.
3. Treat `root-travel`, floor/yoga/Pilates, walking, jumping, props/contact, sitting, kneeling, and rolling/crawling as non-user-facing until they have their own recorded proof and Game visual proof. Keep `root-turn` scoped to standing root orientation only; it is not root travel, walking, turning while traveling, or full locomotion support. `sitting` is the first selected handoff family, but it is still not user-facing and its opt-in validation rows are currently blocking.
4. For the seated bundle, keep the next recording/review target explicit: seated forward fold, with Replay visual capture plus Game visual proof for `strongest-seated-forward-fold`; keep neutral, chair/contact, twist, and leg-lift proof available as regression context.
5. Capture or identify a real walking/root-travel bundle; keep `walking` blocked until `root-travel` has analyzer evidence, passed recorded proof, and the Game plan/review contains readable `strongest-root-travel`. The current audit-generated first command is `npx -p node@22.13.0 npm run movement:replay:validate-scenario -- --scenario movement-proof-root-travel --quiet` after the fresh recording is available in the reviewed recording plan/export.
6. Keep the refreshed 50-target Game visual proof set current after any motion-frame, support-presentation, Replay Lab, Game visual route, or avatar-rendering change, then rerun the focused reviewed analysis/manifest refresh, focused movement tests, and `movement:architecture-guard`.
7. Decide whether to keep only compact proof summaries in tracked documentation or promote a reviewed artifact bundle; do not commit raw `tmp/movement-replay-lab/**` captures by default.
8. Pick the next movement family only after writing its support-claim audit shape first: required recorded proof cases, required Game visual cases, acceptable user-facing copy, and fallback behavior when tracking is uncertain.
9. Before merge or push, run the repo local gate under Node 22.13.0: `npm run verify:env`, `npm run lint:all`, `npm run check`, `npm run build`, and `git diff --check`.

Use these focused commands before broad suites:

```bash
npm run movement:replay:validate-scenario -- --scenario <fresh-recording-label> --quiet
npm run movement:replay:scenario-summary
npm run movement:replay:compare
```

Use the full local gate only before asking to merge or push:

```bash
npm run verify:env
npm run lint:all
npm run check
npm run build
git diff --check
```

## Stop Conditions

Stop and reopen the relevant task if any of these happen:

- A user-facing movement looks wrong even though analyzer status says pass.
- A movement family is being promoted without recorded replay analyzer proof, visual capture, and Game Studio parity proof.
- A Game-only or Replay-only movement decision appears after `MovementSourceFrame`.
- A renderer extraction changes visible motion without focused before/after proof.
- A proof decision file is missing context, stale, or converts source limitations into passes.
